# Cone — Backlog Board

Process: see [WORKFLOW.md](./WORKFLOW.md). Row format: `#Priority Title · size · model · context`.
Status columns: **Icebox → Ready → In Progress → Done**. Only items with a `plans/NN-*.md` are Ready. A shipped plan keeps a `> ✅ Done: <commit>` marker under its title and stays in `plans/` (see WORKFLOW.md "Plan lifecycle").
**Line counts on this board are RAW lines, blanks included** (`(Get-Content f).Count`) — adopted 2026-07-26 because rows had been mixing raw and non-blank counts, which manufactured phantom "it grew" edits (#99 read 846 in one row and 892 in another for the same file; `Servicos.jsx` is **412 non-blank / 452 raw**, re-measured 2026-07-26 — this worked example was itself stale within hours of being written, which is the point). ⚠️ **`Measure-Object -Line` DROPS blank lines** and is what produces the low "phantom" numbers — use `(Get-Content f).Count`. Re-measure before quoting.
🔴 **UNIT DISCONTINUITY, 2026-07-27 — a raw-line figure written before that date is NOT comparable to one written after.** `9b82015` ([plans/49](./plans/49-prettier-format-baseline.md), #24) reformatted 166 files at `printWidth: 100`, taking `cone/src/**/*.{js,jsx}` from **24,259 to 42,837 raw lines — ×1.77 repo-wide — with zero behaviour change.** Nothing rebased this board's numbers at the time, so until 2026-08-04 the #74 watch claimed *"No file is over 800"* while **15 files were**, and `Servicos.jsx`'s worked example directly above ("412 non-blank / 452 raw", 2026-07-26) reads **1199** today. ⚠️ **The multiplier is NOT uniform, so old figures cannot be scaled — only re-measured**: `Publicador.jsx` ×3.34 · `Atletas.jsx` ×2.27 · `Timer.jsx` ×1.37. The spread is structural, not noise — `React.createElement` files (Publicador, AgendaView, events) inflate most because each nested call argument lands on its own line, already-narrow JSX least. This is the second time a stale size figure cost a session: [plans/62](./plans/62-criador-decomposition.md) opened against a recorded "840 raw" and found 1198.
Review findings feeding this board: [reviews/2026-07-02.md](./reviews/2026-07-02.md), [reviews/2026-07-03-rls-probe.md](./reviews/2026-07-03-rls-probe.md), [reviews/2026-07-04-feature-ideas.md](./reviews/2026-07-04-feature-ideas.md), [reviews/2026-07-04-full-pass.md](./reviews/2026-07-04-full-pass.md), [reviews/2026-07-05-full-pass.md](./reviews/2026-07-05-full-pass.md), [reviews/2026-07-05-fourth-pass.md](./reviews/2026-07-05-fourth-pass.md), [reviews/2026-07-09-design-benchmark.md](./reviews/2026-07-09-design-benchmark.md), [reviews/2026-07-16-full-pass.md](./reviews/2026-07-16-full-pass.md), [reviews/2026-07-18.md](./reviews/2026-07-18.md), [reviews/2026-07-26.md](./reviews/2026-07-26.md), [reviews/2026-08-03-criador-text-logging.md](./reviews/2026-08-03-criador-text-logging.md), **[reviews/2026-08-05-full-pass.md](./reviews/2026-08-05-full-pass.md)** (the Tier 3 closeout gate — closed the housekeeping program and ranked Tier 4).
Product docs (personas/tiers/feature catalog/mobile): [FEATURES.md](./FEATURES.md) · [PRODUCT.md](./PRODUCT.md) · [MOBILE.md](./MOBILE.md). Design-pass program umbrella: [plans/16](./plans/16-design-pass-program.md).

> have no top chrome — `schedule.html`'s #147 fix correctly uses `top: 0` on a `.stickyHead` wrapper.
> **#95 owns the token; #144 copies `top: 0`.** Nothing to coordinate.

- 🟢 **[→ Ready · plans/70](./plans/70-backlog-marker-audit.md)** — **#151 Board/plan marker drift, made mechanical** · **XS** · **Sonnet** · Tier 4 rank 4 — cheap, and it protects every session after it. `scripts/audit-backlog-markers.mjs`, same shape as the four existing `audit-*.mjs` (read-only, prints a table) but the **only one needing no Supabase client and no `.env`** — it reads `BACKLOG.md` + `plans/*.md` off disk. Reports the **four measured drift shapes** by name: `ready-but-shipped` · `bare-but-shipped` · `plan-missing-done-marker` · `partial-marker`. ⚠️ **Not a CI gate** (the row says so) — it runs in the session ritual, and a false positive must never block a commit. 🔑 **The verification that matters is proving it can still SEE** — a clean run against the current tree is expected (all ten instances were hand-corrected 2026-08-05), so each shape must be reproduced once in a scratch copy.
- 🟢 **[→ Ready · plans/71](./plans/71-billing-extraction.md)** — **#149 + #104(c) The billing pair** · **S** · **Sonnet** · Tier 4 rank 5. Extract `calcTotal` (`events.jsx:647-658`, **not** `:651`) into a new dependency-free `publicador/billing.js`, invent `sumByCurrency`, test both — then put the rate snapshot on top of a tested function. 🔑 **`calcTotal` closes over nothing** — it reads only its two parameters — so the extraction is a cut-and-paste plus an `export`, with zero call-site rewiring. 🔴 **A live bug falls out for free: #104(b) fixed the PDF only** — `events.jsx:1570-1585`, the on-screen preview grand total, still sums `t.total` across groups with no currency key and no label, so a two-currency report prints a correct per-currency footer in the PDF and a meaningless number in the modal above it; one shared `sumByCurrency` fixes it by construction. **#104(c) = an event-level `rateSnapshot`, with the versioned-history half deferred to #59** (see the decision above and the Icebox row).

> **Four decisions were taken in that planning session so no execution session re-litigates them:**
> **(1) The per-box theme lives in `settings.value.boxThemes`, not on the location row** — `locations`
> is anon-locked by `0006`, so a public page cannot read it at all; same reasoning `useBoxWarnings.js`
> already records for #53. **(2) A visitor's own theme pick is a SEPARATE localStorage key
> (`cone_theme_user`) and always beats the server default** — which is what lets a box default apply to
> a first-time visitor without ever overriding someone who has chosen. **(3) #89 is widened, not
> duplicated** — the user's mobile report covers the Resultados button and scroll-to-top as well, and
> filing a second number for one code path would fragment it. **(4) #71's blocked decision is taken:**
> a **cap of 20** plus a **name-collision confirmation modal** suggesting an initial ("Fulano da Silva"
> → "Fulano S."), never blind dedupe — two real guests can share a first name and both must survive.
>
> 🔴 **#142 gates #143** and is inside plans/67 for that reason: `Publicador.jsx`'s mount effect
> rewrites the whole `settings` blob from its own 45 keys with no read-merge, so it would silently eat
> `boxThemes` on the coach's next Publicador visit — as it has been eating `boxWarnings` all along.

- *(original row, for the record)* — step 1 of the **result fidelity chain** (#115 → #112 → #116 → #117). Qualifies as housekeeping under the 2026-07-26 scope call — it is a user-facing bug fix in a shipped feature, and the bug is live: **9 of 16 logged times in prod (56.3%) have no colon** ([reviews/115-results-audit.md](./reviews/115-results-audit.md)), so `toSecs` reads them as raw seconds — `1400` ranks as 23:20, `3600` as 60:00. `MaskedTimeInput`/`maskMMSS` shipped in #54/C0 and reached **zero** result-logging forms (#35's un-run rollout). Extracts `ScoreFields.jsx` (`ScaleRow`/`RpeRow`/`ScoreInputs`) into `src/public/shared/`, adopts it in `LogForm`/`LogPane`/`DeskRegPane`/`RegistroView`/`ClassPanel`, adds **`expandMMSS`** (blur-time `'14'` → `'14:00'`, the half `maskMMSS`'s right-fill cannot cover), repairs the corrupt prod rows via a print-the-SQL script, kills the `getPerformanceStr` fork, and fixes `ClassPanel`'s non-canonical `'Rx'/'Sc'/'Adp'` scales at source (0 such rows in prod — no cleanup needed). **Absorbs the result-form half of #35.**

- ✅ **[shipped 2026-07-29 · plans/53](./plans/53-block-entry-durability.md)** — **#118 Block-entry durability — the five logging writers must preserve unknown keys** · **S→M** · **Closed:** `src/public/lib/resultEntry.js` (`ATHLETE_KEYS` + `mergeBlockEntry` + `clearAthleteKeys`), adopted by all five writers (`Results.jsx`, `Schedule.jsx`'s `LogPane`/`DeskRegPane`, `RegistroView.jsx`, `useLiveRegistration.js`). `changeLogAthId` no longer spreads the outgoing athlete's `...b`; `useLiveRegistration`'s `rpe: null` clobber is gone; `RegistroView.saveLog` merges into the existing blocks array instead of a wholesale replace. Live-verified against the local Supabase stack: injected a synthetic unknown key into a `results_v2` row, re-logged from `results.html` and `schedule.html`, confirmed it survived both round-trips while the legitimately-changed field updated. 603 tests / lint 0 / `build:all` clean. **This unblocked #112 (shipped same week, see above).**

- *(original row, for the record)* — **run this before #112 and #116; both are blocked on it.** Found while planning them (2026-07-28): **not one of the five logging surfaces preserves unknown keys on a block entry** — every writer builds a fresh object literal with a fixed key whitelist, so a `checkpoint` (#112) or `exerciseRows` (#116) would be written by the athlete and silently destroyed by the next re-log from any other surface. `results_v2.blocks` is `jsonb` and `log_result` passes it through as opaque `p_blocks`, so **the DB carries any key; the JS is what drops it.** Worst case `RegistroView.saveLog:212` — `blocks: presence === 'Presente' ? blockLogs : []` — a wholesale array replacement that also drops entries for non-WOD blocks and for blocks since deleted in Criador, and writes `[]` on a presence change. Two traps in the fix, both recorded in the plan: `Schedule.jsx:553` `changeLogAthId` spreads `...b` (the **outgoing** athlete's form entry), so a new key leaks athlete A's data into athlete B's submission; and `useLiveRegistration.js:41` hardcodes `rpe: null`, so **TV registration already destroys a logged RPE today** — this item fixes that, which is why it is independently valuable rather than pure groundwork. Introduces `src/public/lib/resultEntry.js` (`ATHLETE_KEYS` declared once + `mergeBlockEntry` + `clearAthleteKeys`). Read side is already fine — `blockEntries` spreads `...br`. Same family as #76/#109/#111.

### Captured 2026-08-05 (planning session for plans/69–71)

- **#154 Rate history — re-pricing events that were already booked** · **M** · Sonnet · **the deferred half of #104(c), split out by user decision 2026-08-05. Belongs to #59's Agenda/Publicador design pass, not to its own session.** [plans/71](./plans/71-billing-extraction.md) ships an event-level `rateSnapshot` written at booking time, which stops the bleeding — but a snapshot **categorically cannot re-price events booked before it shipped**, so every pre-existing event keeps pricing at whatever the location's rate is *today*. Regenerating February's Relatório in July still prices February at July's rate for those. The fix that does reach them is a versioned `loc.rateHistory = [{rate, rateUnit, currency, from}, …]` resolved by the event's date — which reaches into `Servicos.jsx`'s `saveLoc` (append a version, don't overwrite `rate`), `startEdit` (read the head), `rateLabel` (resolve "current"), and gives every rate reader an as-of date. ⚠️ **Two constraints:** `Servicos.jsx` today contains **zero** billing arithmetic and writes straight from its mutators per CLAUDE.md's "a load/read path never writes" rule — adding date resolution must not reintroduce a `useEffect` on `locs`; and `currency` is a **free-text input** with no validation, so `'R$'` and `'R$ '` are distinct buckets in any grouping. 🔑 **Run it on top of plans/71's tested `billing.js`**, never before — that sequencing is the whole point of #149.
- **#153 Phone back button logic overhaul** · **M–L** · **Opus** · **user request, 2026-08-05.** ⚠️ **This is a decision before it is a build** — it collides head-on with a recorded "do not re-litigate" call (*"No React Router — URL params are sufficient at current scale"*), so the plan's first job is to establish whether that call still holds now that the app has ~10 pages, 9 SPA tabs and a dozen dismissible surfaces. Current state, measured 2026-08-05:
  - 🔴 **The SPA has ZERO history integration.** `App.jsx`'s `tab` is `useState` (seeded from `location.search` by a lazy initializer per plans/51), so the Android back gesture from **any** of the 9 tabs **exits the app entirely** — there is no way back to the tab you came from, and no `pushState`/`popstate` anywhere in `src/components/`.
  - **`me.html` is the only page with real history integration**, and it is the model: a `popstate` listener (`Me.jsx:131-132`), `pushState` on athlete select (`:186`) and on clearing (`:195`), `replaceState` to seed `?id=` without a spurious entry (`:167`).
  - `Schedule.jsx:444` uses `replaceState` once, but only to strip params — not navigation. `Timer.jsx:452,460` calls `history.back()` as its own "exit", which **assumes** the timer was launched from `schedule.html`; opened directly (or via a shared link) back leaves the site.
  - 🔴 **The dismissible-surface problem is the bigger half.** There are **10 `<ConfirmReview>` call sites** plus the bottom-sheet families (`me/Sheet.jsx`, the global `ex-sheet*` classes used by Criador's exercise row **and** `BoxWarnings`, `CheckinSheet`, `SessionMetaModal`, `PrLogSheet`, `BodySheet`) and Criador's own editor. **Escape is handled in 8 files — but the Android back gesture is NOT Escape**, so on a phone none of these close on back: back leaves the page outright with the sheet still open. That is the behaviour the overhaul is really about.
  - **Decide per surface class, not globally** — and note two constraints up front: a history entry pushed for a modal must be popped when the modal is closed *by other means* (or the stack fills with phantom entries), and `tv.html`/`timer.html` are launched-and-left surfaces where a back trap would be worse than the status quo. ⚠️ **Do not adopt a router as the opening move** — `me.html`'s existing `pushState`/`popstate` pair is the proof that the primitive is enough here; the plan should price both.

### Captured 2026-08-05 (user request)

- **#152 Explore locking mobile orientation to portrait, and whether zoom should be blocked too** · **S (spike, then a decision)** · Sonnet · **user-requested exploration, not a decided change** — the output is a recommendation plus whatever part of it is worth doing. Current state, measured 2026-08-05: `manifest.json:10` is **`"orientation": "any"`**, there is **not one `@media (orientation: …)` rule in the repo**, and the entry pages carry **three different viewport metas** — 10 pages on `width=device-width,initial-scale=1.0`, one on `…,viewport-fit=cover`, and **`tv.html` on `width=1920`** (correct for the wall, and the reason this must not be a blanket change). ⚠️ **Three constraints to establish before writing any code, because two of them make the naive fix a no-op and the third makes it a regression:** (a) **`screen.orientation.lock()` requires fullscreen and is unsupported on iOS Safari entirely** — the only lever that works there is the *installed-PWA* manifest, so `"orientation": "portrait"` helps an Android install and does nothing in a mobile browser tab; a CSS `@media (orientation: landscape)` "gire o aparelho" overlay is the only in-tab option, and it is a **suggestion, not a lock**. (b) **`user-scalable=no` has been deliberately ignored by iOS Safari since iOS 10**, so a viewport-meta zoom block is largely theatre on the platform it would matter most on. (c) 🔴 **Blocking zoom is a WCAG 1.4.4 failure** (text must resize to 200%) and would work against **#14**, which is already on the board — so the honest default is *don't block it*, and treat "users pinch-zoom to read" as a symptom of **#95** (226px topbar), **#144** (selectors scroll away) and **#146** (two-up PR cards on a 390px phone). ⚠️ **`tv.html` and `timer.html` must be excluded** whatever is decided — the wall display is landscape by definition and the timer is routinely propped sideways. Decide per surface, not globally.

  🔑 **MEASURED 2026-08-05, and it splits this row in two — the halves are NOT the same item:**
  - **The zoom half dissolves into the layout rows and should be closed with them.** Blocking zoom is the wrong fix (WCAG 1.4.4, and iOS ignores the meta anyway); people pinch-zoom because of **#95**, **#144**, **#145** and **#146**. Ship those and re-ask. Nothing to build here.
  - 🔴 **The orientation half does NOT dissolve, and the reason is structural: rotating a phone crosses the 768px desktop breakpoint.** A 390×844 phone in landscape is an **844×390 viewport**, so `@media(min-width:768px)` fires and the page swaps to the layout built for a ~900px-tall desktop window. Verified live on `schedule.html` at 844×390: it renders the **full three-pane desktop view** — a 220px sidebar (26% of the width), the week strip, the athlete list and the session pane — inside 390px of height, with the sidebar's own nav clipped ("Tema" cut off, "Coach" not reachable). It is not broken so much as hostile, and **no portrait layout fix can reach it. Eleven public CSS modules carry a `min-width:768px` desktop layout**, so this is app-wide, not one page. The real question this row should answer is therefore **not "can we lock rotation"** (mostly no — see the constraints above) **but "should the ≥768px branch be gated on width AND height, or on `(orientation: landscape) and (max-height: …)`, so a rotated phone keeps the mobile layout instead of inheriting the desktop one."** That is a real, implementable change and it is where the value is.

### Captured 2026-08-06 (user request)

- **#155 iOS Safari auto-zooms when logging a result, and the confirm modal isn't centered afterward** · **S** · Sonnet · **user-reported from an iPhone — not yet reproduced on-device, but the mechanism has a concrete match in the code, recorded here rather than guessed at.** `ScoreFields.module.css:128-138`'s `.input` — the plain result field shared by `results.html`'s `LogForm`, `schedule.html`'s `LogPane`/`DeskRegPane` and `RegistroView` (per the file's own header comment) — is `font-size:13px`, under the 16px floor iOS Safari uses to decide whether to auto-zoom a focused control, a rule `user-scalable=no` does not suppress (the same finding **#152** already recorded). Its sibling `MaskedTimeInput.module.css:14` is already `16px` and zoom-safe — the same fix CLAUDE.md documents for the SPA's `ui/Input` ("16px value = sem zoom no iOS") — so `.input` is the one score field that never got it. `ConfirmReview`'s overlay (`ConfirmReview.module.css:2-11`, `position:fixed;inset:0` centered via flex) centers against the layout viewport; once iOS auto-zooms, the visual viewport it actually paints into is scaled and offset from that — the textbook cause of a `position:fixed` overlay drifting off-center on iOS specifically, matching "zooms the screen and changes the modal placing." Fix candidate: raise `.input` to `16px`, the same one-line shape as `MaskedTimeInput`'s. **Re-verify the modal centering on-device after that alone** — the zoom is the trigger, but stale-viewport `position:fixed` drift is a known separate iOS quirk that sometimes needs its own fix (e.g. reading `visualViewport` on open), so don't assume the font-size change closes both symptoms.

- **#144 `results.html`'s athlete and week selectors scroll away; `schedule.html`'s don't** · **S** · Sonnet · `Results.module.css` contains **zero** occurrences of `sticky` — `.selBar` (`:23`) and `.weekNav` (`:27`) are plain flow, so on a phone the athlete `<select>` and the week arrows leave the screen as soon as the results list scrolls, and changing either means scrolling back to the top of the page. `Schedule.jsx` solves the same problem on the same viewport (`Schedule.module.css:25,35,37` — `.hdr` `top:0`, `.selBar` `top:88px`, `.weekNav` `top:136px`) and the two pages are otherwise siblings, so this is drift rather than a considered difference. ⚠️ **Schedule's offsets are hand-stacked magic numbers, not a token** — `--spa-sticky-top` (`index.css:37`, with a `min-width:768px` override at `:793`) exists for exactly this and is used by `criador.module.css:26`, but **no public page references it**. Copying `88px`/`136px` into a third file entrenches the literal; the row should decide whether to promote the token to the public pages first. Desktop is unaffected (both pages hide these bars via `.mobileOnly` and scroll their panes internally).
  🔴 **THIS ROW'S SCHEDULE CITATION IS NOW STALE, and its token question is ANSWERED — measured 2026-08-05 while planning [plans/69](./plans/69-spa-chrome.md).** (a) `2b0a387` (**#147**) **deleted** all three of `.hdr` `top:0` / `.selBar` `top:88px` / `.weekNav` `top:136px`; `Schedule.module.css:25-36` is now the #147 comment and `:37` is `.stickyHead{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:20}`, a single wrapper hoisted out of `<main>` as a direct child of `.inner`. **That wrapper is the pattern to copy** — it deletes the magic numbers rather than promoting them. (b) **The token question resolves to NO:** `--spa-sticky-top` is **SPA-chrome-only**, public pages have no top chrome, and Schedule's fix correctly uses `top: 0`. #144 copies `top: 0`; **#95 owns the token** and there is nothing to coordinate. (c) ⚠️ **"both pages hide these bars via `.mobileOnly`" is FALSE for Results' `.weekNav`** — `Results.jsx:675` has no `.mobileOnly`, because Results has **no desktop week-nav equivalent**; `.weekNav` *is* the desktop week navigation, sitting inside `.main`'s `overflow:hidden` flex column as a `flex-shrink:0` row. So hoisting it into a `.stickyHead` **is** a desktop layout change here, unlike on Schedule. - **#146 PR cards are two-up at every width, including a 390px phone** · **XS** · Sonnet · `Me.module.css:185` — `.tileGrid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr)) }`, with **no media query anywhere in the file** overriding it. So a PR card gets ~180px on a phone, which is what makes #145's squeeze visible and what truncates long movement names. The user's call is one card per row. Render site `PrSection.jsx:211`; the same grid serves both `BenchTile` and `MovTile`. ⚠️ **Two corrections, measured 2026-08-05:** (a) *"no media query anywhere in the file"* is **false as written** — `Me.module.css` has **two** (`:6` layout fork, `:321` typography), and the second one restyles `.tileName`/`.tileNum`/`.benchName`/`.benchTime`. The defensible claim is *"no media query overrides `.tileGrid`"*, which is true (it appears exactly once, at `:185`). (b) **This will not change benchmark tiles** — `.benchTile{grid-column: 1 / -1}` (`:222`) already makes them full-width, so "one card per row" is a **MovTile-only** change in practice. ✅ **Both #145 and #146 are gallery-covered** (`gallery/groups/me.jsx:508-541`, item `prsection`, 3 cases) — Lane A, and the fixtures already reach every state incl. a regressed PR and a target-less one.

### From the 2026-08-03 targeted review ([reviews/2026-08-03-criador-text-logging.md](./reviews/2026-08-03-criador-text-logging.md))

*Dimensions 1+2 only, scoped to Criador text mode and the public logging chain. **Found by driving,
not reading** — the round-trip numbers come from running the real serializer+parser over all 339 prod
blocks in 71 sessions, and every UX claim was reproduced live at 1280 and 390. All 13 land in
**Tier 3** under the 2026-07-26 housekeeping scope call. **#126–#130 are input to the #120/#121
planning session**, not separate work to schedule ahead of it. ⚠️ Two figures in the report were wrong
on first measurement and re-derived before being written down (a loose CSS selector, and a
`JSON.stringify` comparison that counted key order) — the rows below carry the corrected ones.*

- ✅ **[shipped 2026-08-03 · plans/60](./plans/60-goal-time-input.md)** — **#125 A `Meta:` time typed without a colon is stored as SECONDS, and it inverts #117's goal badge** · **S** · **Closed:** the `expandMMSS`-on-blur contract moved OFF `TimeField` and INTO `MaskedTimeInput` itself, so `GoalInput.jsx`'s two direct-`MaskedTimeInput` fields (and `StationEditor.jsx`'s `restBetweenCycles`) get it for free — **`GoalInput.jsx` needed zero code changes.** ⚠️ **CORRECTION 2026-08-04: "zero code changes" was the bug.** The *blur* path is fixed and the one prod row was repaired, but `GoalInput.jsx:41` persists **every keystroke** (`onChange={v => put({ min: v })}`), so a value that never receives a blur is still written as `min:'14'` — filed as **#139**, whose evidence is a prod row created *after* this shipped. **The write path is still open**; do not read this row as closing the input. Live-verified end to end against the real prod-seeded row (`msclpzw5gcc3sna588q`, "Eagles Monday Test"): typed/blurred → `14` → `14:00`, `goalStr` now renders `14'`, and logging a 5:00 result against the (now-correct) 14:00 goal made #117's "Bateu a meta" badge render on both the leaderboard and the success modal — it could never have rendered against the old 14-*second* goal, for any athlete. **Second half (`GoalInput.jsx:20`'s kind-mismatch empty-field behavior) deliberately NOT touched** — the gallery's own "Tipo trocado" case documents that as intentional (a changed block type must not show a stale goal); the actual invisible-corruption risk is the text-mode round-trip flipping `kind` unexpectedly, filed as **#126** and owned by the #120/#121 Opus session. **Folded in one more `#35` rollout item:** `me/PrLogSheet.jsx`'s time-PR field had **zero** masking and fed the same `toSecs` bug into the athlete's own PR-best comparison (`Me.jsx:249`) — fixed by wiring `maskMMSS`/`expandMMSS` directly onto its existing input (not a `MaskedTimeInput` swap, which would have visibly broken its distinctive large-numeral `.lsInp` styling shared with the reps/kg/m variants). **`Timer.jsx`'s own colonless-goal gap deliberately left alone** — traced `cfg.goal` to confirm it's display-only (never reaches `toSecs`/ranking), so the bug there is cosmetic, and #108's live-gym-timer caution outweighs a cosmetic fix. Prod repair: `scripts/repair-goal-time.mjs` (mirrors `normalize-session-ids.mjs` — dry-run default, `--write` local-only, prints `jsonb_set` SQL for prod) re-confirmed the 1-row count live against prod (not the stale local seed) and printed the SQL, handed to the user to paste into the SQL editor. 645 tests / lint 0 / `build:all` clean; the 3 design-card diffs `design:cards` produced were pure pre-existing date-drift noise (`WeekImportModal`/`WeekGrid` "today"-relative fixtures) and were reverted, same precedent as #24.

What is still broken is `rounds` (`reps` dropped, `min` string→number) and `text` promoted to `time`. (2) **"A note containing `Meta:` is eaten" does NOT reproduce** — `RE_META` is anchored at `^`, and `Obs: Ou 1600m. Meta: 8 minutos.` round-trips with the note intact and `goal: undefined`. ### Found while executing plans/61·B (2026-08-04)

- ✅ **[shipped 2026-08-04 · plans/63](./plans/63-criador-keystroke-writes.md)** — ⚠️ **CORRECTION at execution: this row's "Two candidate fixes, pick deliberately" is wrong — they are COMPLEMENTS, not alternatives, and each alone leaves a live hole.** `normalizeGoal` at the `saveS` boundary cannot cover the Texto pane (it serializes **unsaved** blocks, so the flip-to-text path never reaches a save), and `serializeGoal` cannot cover `toSecs`/`goalStr`/`goalOutcome` (nothing that reads the goal for ranking or display goes through the serializer). Both shipped. See Done. — **#139 `MaskedTimeInput` writes a partial mm:ss through on every keystroke, so a `Meta:` typed as `14` is still stored as `{kind:'time', min:'14'}`** · **S** · **surfaced by `scripts/audit-text-roundtrip.mjs`, which reads `goal-kind: 1` against live prod on a row created 2026-08-03 — i.e. AFTER [plans/60](./plans/60-goal-time-input.md) shipped.** ⚠️ **This corrects an assumption in 61·A:** A2 pinned the `{kind:'time', min:'14'}` shape as a regression guard on the stated basis that *"input closed, prod row repaired"*. The **prod row** was repaired; the **input was not**. plans/60 moved the `expandMMSS`-on-blur contract into `MaskedTimeInput`, but `GoalInput.jsx`'s `onChange={v => put({ min: v })}` persists every intermediate value, so typing `14` and clicking away leaves `min: '14'` in storage whenever blur doesn't land on the field the way plans/60's live test did. Round trip: `serializeGoal` emits a bare `Meta: 14` (its `short()` only shortens a `:00`-suffixed value), and `parseGoal` refuses to read a naked integer as a time (`if (one && /['":]/.test(s))`) — so it comes back `{kind:'text', text:'14'}` and **#117's "Bateu a meta" badge stops rendering for that block**, the exact failure plans/60 was written to kill. Two candidate fixes, pick deliberately: normalize the legacy bare-minutes shape in `serializeGoal` (one line, makes the round trip lossless but leaves the wrong value in storage), or stop `GoalInput` persisting an unexpanded value (fixes the source; touches the field every `Meta:` goes through). **Blast radius today: 1 prod block.** Deliberately **not** fixed by 61·B — it is a goal-serialization row in 61·A's family, and 61·B's scope was stations + the locked passthrough.

### Found while closing plans/63 (2026-08-04)

- **#141 One prod goal stores `min` as a JS number, not a string** · **XS** · Sonnet · **Tier 4** (it needs a call on whether to repair the row, harden the readers, or both). `2026-07-29 #2 AMRAP "Eagles Wednesday Test"` holds `{"min": 4, "kind": "rounds"}`. Surfaced by `audit-text-roundtrip.mjs`'s *"goals com `min` numérico em storage"* counter, which is **separate from `goal-kind`** and stayed at 1 after plans/63's repair took that one to 0. Same family as **#110** (session ids) and the correction on **#126**, which named exactly this shape — 61·A made `parseGoal` keep `min` a **string**, so nothing writes a number today and this is a pre-61·A leftover. **Benign right now**: every reader coerces (`goalOutcome` via `Number()`, `serializeGoal`'s rounds branch via template interpolation, `GoalInput`'s `value={g?.min ?? ''}` on a number input). ⚠️ **The hazard is the next `.startsWith`/`.endsWith` added to a goal field** — `serializeGoal`'s time branch used to call `goal.min.endsWith(':00')` directly and would have thrown on a number; plans/63 incidentally hardened it by running `expandMMSS` (which stringifies) first. One row, so a `jsonb_set` one-liner like plans/60's, or a coercion in `normalizeGoal`, or both.

### From the 2026-08-04 user bug report

- ✅ **[shipped 2026-08-04 · plans/63](./plans/63-criador-keystroke-writes.md)** — 🔴 **One thing the plan got wrong and execution caught: the draft must sync against what the field LAST SENT UP, not against the derived `customName`.** The plan said "same shape as `ExerciseCombobox`'s `query`" — but that component's parent stores back what it writes, while this one **transforms** it (`label` collapses to an empty `customName` the moment it equals the type), so a plain `prev !== prop` sync resyncs the draft to `''` on that very keystroke and defeats the draft in the exact case it exists for. Caught by reasoning through "WOD" typed into a WOD block *before* running it. See Done. — **#140 The Criador's block-name field swallows the space bar** · **S** · **Sonnet** · **user-reported from live use, and root-caused at capture.** The coach's account: *"complex names do not register the space bar the first time — I have to write everything then go back and add the spaces."* `BlockEditor.jsx:400-402` is `onChange={e => onUpdate({ ...block, label: e.target.value.trim() || block.type })}` — **`.trim()` runs on every keystroke**, so the trailing space of `Aquecimento ` is stripped before it can be typed: press space, nothing appears; type `g`, you get `Aquecimentog`. Mid-string spaces survive a trim, which is exactly why typing the name first and inserting spaces afterwards works. 🔎 **This is the only trim-on-change input in `src/`** (verified repo-wide), so it is one field, not a class. The `|| block.type` half is *why* the trim is there (an empty custom name falls back to the type) and carries **a second defect of the same shape**: `customName` (`:47`) derives to `''` whenever `block.label === block.type`, so typing a name that *equals* the type (`WOD` on a WOD block) blanks the field mid-word. Fix is a local draft synced during render — `ExerciseCombobox.jsx:45-49`'s `prevValue`/`setPrevValue` shape, which is what `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` requires (a `useEffect` here fails CI) — committing raw on change and normalized on blur. One draft fixes both. **Rides plans/63** because it is #139's root shape seen from another angle.

### Found while planning plans/61 (2026-08-03)

- **#138 An exercise's volume metric should be `reps | distância | tempo`, not a unit-bearing string in `reps`** · **S–M** · **raised by the user while reviewing plans/61; plan it WITH #93, not alone.** A hold is stored today as `reps: '40"'` — a numeric-looking field carrying a unit, the same debt class as **#93** (`block.duration` bare minutes) and **#110** (session ids). **Measured against prod: 7 of 1059 exercises carry a time in `reps`, and 5 of those are `Rest`** — so only **2** real movements (`30" LSit argola`, `40" flutter kicks`), and `exVolStr` renders all 7 correctly. **It is modelling debt, not a live bug**, which is why [plans/61](./plans/61-criador-text-roundtrip.md)'s 61·A fixes the two *parse positions* that were destroying these exercises (#130) and deliberately leaves the *shape* alone. A real fix touches `ExerciseRow`'s four layouts, `ExerciseList`'s four sizes, `exVolStr`, registry `defaults{}`, TV, schedule, results and `textFormat` — i.e. it is a migration, not a field rename. ⚠️ Do **not** take the "store it as a comment for now" shortcut: the current `reps` representation parses and renders correctly end to end, so a comment would be a downgrade.

- **#95 The SPA topbar is 226px tall on a phone and overflows horizontally** · **S–M** · Sonnet · **measured live at 390×844, affects every SPA tab.** `.topbar` renders **226px** (27% of an 844px viewport) before any tab content, because its five state buttons — Sair · Sincronizar · Limpar estado · Carregar estado · Salvar estado — stack vertically at `x=199`, `y=33→215` instead of collapsing. `document.scrollWidth` 387 > `clientWidth`, so the page also **scrolls sideways** and the account email is clipped mid-word. This is SPA **chrome**, not a tab, so no C-session owns it and every mobile tab pays for it. **Fold in the dev-leftover half:** Limpar/Carregar/Salvar estado are localStorage debug affordances sitting in the production topbar — the same family as the "Salvar config.json" leftover #55 called for removing. Decide per button: keep behind a menu, or drop. **✅ DESIGN SETTLED 2026-07-26 (coach decisions — this can be planned cold; it is the next item after plans/39–41):**
  1. **State buttons** — `Sincronizar` **stays** in the topbar as an **icon-only** button (it carries a live conflict state and must remain one tap away). `Salvar estado` / `Carregar estado` / `Limpar estado` **move into a "Dados" card in Configurações**. The active tab is already underlined in the nav, so it **is** the title; a separate title line is redundant. This merges today's `.topbar` + `.tab-bar` into a single sticky row: **~275px → ~48px** (226px wrapped topbar + 49px tab-bar), and it **eliminates the `.tab-bar{top:44px}` coupling** — that hardcode is an assumption about the topbar's height that breaks the moment it wraps, i.e. it *is* half this bug.
  3. **Scope — both widths.** Desktop keeps its 220px sidebar for nav, so there the topbar only loses the state buttons and gets the icon-only Início/Sair. This also kills today's **duplicate Início** (sidebar `App.jsx:269` **and** topbar `App.jsx:301`).
  4. **Fold in, found while reading:** `App.jsx:339` is a raw **`window.confirm`** on "Limpar estado" — the **last one in the app** (C4 replaced Criador's). It moves to Configurações with the button and becomes a `ConfirmReview`. Update **`--spa-sticky-top`** (currently 93px mobile / 49px desktop) to the new single-row height.

- ✅ **[shipped 2026-07-27 · plans/50](./plans/50-taxonomy-single-source.md)** *(marker corrected 2026-08-05, see the full pass — it had read "→ Ready" for nine days)* — **#98 The 15-category taxonomy is forked FIVE ways — adding a category would silently create no registry bucket** · **S** · Sonnet · **⚠️ RECOUNTED 2026-07-26: it is 5 list declarations, not 3, and `BLOCK_ORDER` has moved.** CLAUDE.md's rule is "extend `FAMILY_GROUPS`/`ROOTS` in `exerciseGroups.js`, never at a call site", but the same 15 categories are declared at: **`exerciseGroups.js:16-28`** `FAMILY_GROUPS`→`ALL_CATEGORIES` (canonical, family order) · **`components/tabs/exerciciosHelpers.js:3-7`** `BLOCK_ORDER` (*moved out of `Exercicios.jsx:14` in `32063e9` — every doc pointing at the old location is dead*; this is what `initRegistry` iterates) · **`utils/config.js:16`** `APP_CONFIG.blockNames` (16, `'-'` sentinel + `BLOCK_ORDER` verbatim) · **`criador/blockModel.js:73-93`** `TYPE_CONFIG` keys (consumed as `known` by `criador/TypePicker.jsx:13`) · **`exerciseGroups.test.js:8-11`** `CATEGORIES` (a deliberate fixed-contract duplicate, but its comment still cites the dead `Exercicios.jsx` location). **All five hold the same 15 members — the real divergence is ORDER:** `exerciseGroups.js` hoists Benchmark to position 7 and orders the green family Skill→Cardio→Aquecimento→Mobilidade, versus Aquecimento→Skill→Cardio→Mobilidade in the other four. So a category added to `FAMILY_GROUPS` renders in Pane 1 and **holds nothing**, because `initRegistry` never creates its bucket. **Four more parallel category-keyed maps exist in the same domain** and would silently miss a new category too: `config.js:60-84` `BTC`, `:86-110` `PLC`, `:112-137` `ECOL_BASE`, and `wod.js:12-17` `BLOCK_FAMILY`. Fix: `initRegistry` iterates `ALL_CATEGORIES`; `blockNames` derives from it (keeping `'-'` explicitly). ✅ **The row's open question — "verify the persisted registry key order matters to no reader" — is ANSWERED 2026-07-27, and the answer is more interesting than yes or no: a reader DOES depend on key order, and it happens to be immune to this particular change.** `initRegistry` builds the blob by iterating `BLOCK_ORDER`, so JSON key order *is* `BLOCK_ORDER`, and `me/PrSection.jsx:179` renders me.html's PR family cards straight off `Object.keys(registry)` **with no sort**. But it filters through `PR_SKIP` (`meHelpers.js:22` = `{'-','Aquecimento','Descanso',...WOD_TYPES}`) and excludes `BENCHMARK_CAT` separately — and **Aquecimento and Benchmark are exactly the two categories whose relative position differs between the two orders**, so both yield `Força · LPO · Core · Acessórios · Skill · Cardio · Mobilidade`. Record it as *verified-safe-by-coincidence*, **not** as "no reader uses key order": if `PR_SKIP` ever stops skipping Aquecimento the coupling is live again. The other three `Object.entries(registry)` consumers (`registry.js:215`, `Atletas.jsx:37`, `ExerciseCombobox.jsx:18`) build order-insensitive maps, and `exerciseGroups.test.js:15` compares **sorted**, so it neither catches nor blocks an order change.

- 🟡 **(a)(b)(d) shipped 2026-08-05 · [plans/68](./plans/68-tier3-closeout.md); THIS ROW STAYS OPEN FOR (c) only.** ⚠️ **The row's "all in `Servicos.jsx` / `publicador/events.jsx`" was wrong — `Servicos.jsx` holds no billing arithmetic at all**, only the rate form and a display-only `rateLabel`. — **#104 Billing correctness — four bugs in the only money code in the app** · **S** · Sonnet · all in `Servicos.jsx` / `publicador/events.jsx`, none currently tracked. (a) **`per_hour` truncates**: `Math.max(1, Math.floor((ev.durationMin||60)/60))` (`events.jsx:213`) bills a 90-minute class as **one hour**. (b) **Mixed-currency grand total is wrong**: `grandCurrency` is overwritten by whichever group is last (`events.jsx:260`), so two locations with different `currency` strings sum into one meaningless number. (c) **Rates are retroactive**: `calcTotal` reads the location's *current* `rate`, so regenerating February's Relatório in July bills February at July's price — there is no rate history and no rate snapshot on the event. (d) **`pixClean` is duplicated byte-identical** in `utils/pix.js:12` and `publicador/exportHelpers.js:7`, and `events.jsx` imports one copy while `pix.js` uses the other. Also worth noting for whoever picks this up: `pix.test.js` (13 tests) never validates the CRC against a known-good reference payload, only its *format*, and asserts none of the EMV field truncations (25/15/72/25). *(The mount-write-back in the same file is **#109**, not here.)*
- **#105 Agenda has one filter and the filter set already exists twice** · **M** · Sonnet · `AgendaView` offers exactly one tri-state filter (all/scheduled/completed, `:13`), while `ReportModal.filteredEvents` (`events.jsx:175-190`) already implements period + type + status + services + athletes. **Lift that into one shared filter component; do not write a second copy** — this is the same duplication class #37/#48/#51 each cleaned up. Add box/coach/turma once #103/#40 exist. Second half: a **week/list view** — today it is a month grid plus a day pane, with no way to see a run of days as a list. Naturally rides **#59**.
- **#106 Recurrence series cannot be edited or deleted** · **S** · Sonnet · `events.jsx:20-35,116-143` expands a recurrence client-side into N event objects sharing `recurrenceGroup: base.id` — and **nothing ever reads `recurrenceGroup` again.** Generate a weekly class for a quarter and you get ~13 rows that must be deleted one at a time, with no "edit this series" and no "delete from here on". The grouping key is already persisted, so this is UI over existing data.
- **#107 Membership plans / class quotas** · **L** · Opus · the Box-tier feature ("N classes per period per athlete"). **Blocked on #102, not on tenancy or RLS** — a quota is meaningless until attendance is countable, and today it isn't. Athletes carry no plan, tier, quota, or credit field of any kind (an athlete is 7 fields: `id, name, level, goal, notes, color, since`), and membership is stored **inverted** on `locations[].athleteIds`.

### Captured 2026-07-23

- **#88 Reconcile prod vs. dev database — a mismatch was observed** · **S–M** · Sonnet · ✅ **PARTIALLY ANSWERED 2026-08-03 by the targeted review, which hit it while re-seeding: `scripts/seed-dev.mjs` can no longer seed `locations` or `coach_profile` at all, and reports it as "EMPTY".** `seed-dev.mjs:49` builds the prod client with `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`, and `0006_lock_business_reads.sql` (#81) revoked anon read on **exactly those two tables** — so the fetch returns nothing and the script prints `EMPTY locations` / `EMPTY coach_profile`, which is **indistinguishable from "prod genuinely has none"**, then leaves whatever the local stack held from before `0006` landed. Consequence: box tags, `?box=` scoping, box warnings, and the entire Serviços/billing surface (**#104**, the only money code in the app) are developed and verified against **stale or absent** local data. This is at least part of the divergence this row was filed for. Fix is small — the seed needs the prod **service-role** key for those two tables, or an explicit "skipped: anon cannot read since 0006" message instead of `EMPTY` so the gap is never again mistaken for empty data. ⚠️ This does **not** close the row: the schema/policy half is still un-enumerated. *(Original row:)* **captured from the coach, needs investigation before sizing.** A discrepancy was noticed between the **prod** Supabase (`crsalcpvsedmiabkeibp`) and the **local dev** stack (seeded by `scripts/seed-dev.mjs`, ports +10). Unknown yet *what* diverged — schema/policies, or blob-table *data* the seed snapshot didn't capture (the seed reads a fixed table list; anything outside it, or written to prod since the last seed, won't be in dev). First step is to enumerate the actual difference: `supabase db dump --linked --schema public` vs the local schema for structure (⚠️ trust the **dump**, not `db diff --linked` — CLAUDE.md records it silently ignores RLS-policy divergence), and a row/blob compare for data. Then decide whether it's a re-seed (dev stale — most likely, benign), a migration-history gap (prod has objects the migrations don't — see the `0005`/`0006`/`0007` local-only notes), or real prod drift. Do **not** push anything at prod until the direction is known.

- **#82 Normalize `sessions` out of its single JSONB blob (unblocks hidden-session RLS)** · **L** · Opus · split off from #81's structural companion. While all sessions live in one `sessions` blob row, RLS can't protect `public:false` "hidden" sessions — RLS filters rows, not fields inside a blob — so client-side hiding stays bypassable via direct REST, and per-box (#80) scoping can never become real access control. Normalizing sessions into per-row storage (like `results_v2`) is the prerequisite for #30/#31/#40 to actually enforce anything on the session side. Big migration (data move + every read/write path in Criador/public pages) — hence its own L item, not folded into the gating tickets.

Fix: set `test.pool` in `vite.config.js` (and/or add `engines`/`.nvmrc` to pin Node). Found while verifying the test gate in the 2026-07-18 review.

Every mm:ss-shaped field is converted in plans/37 — but `block.duration` is stored as **bare minutes** and read that way by TV, timer, `blkMeta`, `stationsCapStr`, Schedule and Publicador, and `toSecs('14')` reads `14` as **seconds**. ### From the 2026-07-24 C1 session (coach directive)

- ✅ **#94 Audit prod: exercise names used in sessions but absent from the registry** · **S** · **DONE 2026-07-25 (58.5% → 11.9%)** → [`docs/reviews/94-session-registry-audit.md`](./reviews/94-session-registry-audit.md) (regenerate: `node scripts/audit-session-registry.mjs`). **775 name occurrences · 453 unresolved (58.5%) · 343 distinct misses**, bucketed: **130 likely-registerable movements** (the actionable list — Wall Ball ×9, Thruster, Lunge ×6, Squat Clean/Snatch, bare Pull-up, GHD, Devil's Press, Burpee Box Jump Over…), **179 prescription-in-the-name** ("800m Run", "15 GHD", "30 Wall Ball" — the movement is fine, a leading distance/count leaked in; `stripVolumeNoise` only strips a bare leading count, not "800m"/"200m Row" — extend it, don't register these), **26 compound/complex notation** (registry-unfixable by design), **8 structural noise** ("Rest"/"Then"/"Rounds"/"Bloco …" leaked into name fields — data hygiene, not a registry gap). **ROUND 1 SHIPPED 2026-07-25** (coach reviewed the report inline and signed off R1–R5): unresolved **58.5% → 43.4% from resolver changes alone**, and **→ 34.8%** once the registry additions land. Four generalizations in `registry.js` rather than a bigger alias table — extended `stripVolumeNoise` (distance/duration/rep-scheme/per-side/cal/zone/`heavy`), auto-indexed `Movement (SH)` shorthand entries, spaced spellings of every hyphenated entry, and a guarded plural fallback (see CLAUDE.md's `registry.js` bullet for the contract). Root cause of the "Corrida" miss was **not** a backwards alias but a **dangling pointer**: `'run'/'sprint'` both targeted a "Corrida" entry the registry has never had (the real one is `Run`) — two test fixtures had the same fiction baked in and were corrected to prod reality. `scripts/audit-session-registry.mjs` now reports **dangling aliases** against live prod every run, so the class can't recur silently. **Registry additions APPLIED to prod 2026-07-25** ([`docs/reviews/94-registry-additions.sql`](./reviews/94-registry-additions.sql), idempotent/alphabetical) — prod confirmed at Cardio 18 · Skill 27 · Força 24 · Acessórios 31, audit re-run reports **0 dangling aliases**, bucket 1 130 → 102 and bucket 2 179 → 106. **Round 1 closed at 34.8% unresolved (277/795).** **ROUND 2 (2026-07-25)** → [`docs/reviews/94-round2-decisions.md`](./reviews/94-round2-decisions.md). Code half shipped (slot letters `A- 3 Snatch Balance` · trailing tempo/pause notes · trailing parens · alternating suffix · misspellings · audit classifier now files structural labels as noise instead of "register me"), plus the coach's decision batches as aliases: **34.8% → 28.1%**. **Round-2 additions APPLIED to prod 2026-07-25** ([`94-round2-registry-additions.sql`](./reviews/94-round2-registry-additions.sql), 71 entries) — prod confirmed at Core 35 · LPO 34 · Força 33 · Acessórios 51 · Skill 41 · Cardio 19. **#94 CLOSED at 11.9% (95/795), 0 dangling aliases**, bucket 1 130 → 16 and bucket 2 179 → 19. That is effectively the floor: 51 of the 95 remaining are bucket 3 (compound notation) + bucket 4 (structural noise), unresolvable by design, and the rest are deliberately-skipped complex fragments and one-off singles. Going further means changing how compound lines are ENTERED (#92 already flags them `complex-detected`), not growing the registry. Related to #62.

**2026-08-06 — #95 The SPA chrome becomes one 49px row, extracted as a real component** · [plans/69](./plans/69-spa-chrome.md) · `9a24ee8`. Tier 4's third row, and the design program's opener. `src/components/chrome/AppChrome.jsx` + `AppChrome.module.css` + `tabs.js` replaced `App.jsx`'s inline topbar/tab-bar/sidebar (two stacked layouts, up to 226px tall on mobile) with one `min-height:48px` row at both widths — removing `.topbar-right`'s `flex-wrap` was the actual bug fix. **Re-measured live before touching anything** (390×844): topbar 226px unchanged since plans/67, `scrollWidth` 460 vs `clientWidth` 380 (80px of sideways scroll). **After:** chrome 48px, `scrollWidth === clientWidth` at both 390 and 1280, exactly one Início node in the DOM at both widths.

`--spa-sticky-top` (`index.css`) is now a single `49px` declaration with no `@media` override — every consumer (`criador.module.css`'s `.stickyHead`, `Criador.jsx`'s JS fallback, `.rp-sticktop` in Resultados + Agenda mobile) reads the same token unconditionally, closing the four-mutually-inconsistent-heights gap the Tier 4 banner recorded while planning. `Salvar`/`Carregar`/`Limpar estado` moved out of the chrome entirely into a new "Dados" section in Configurações (`src/components/tabs/config/stateBackup.js` — pure `buildSnapshot`/`stateFileName`/`parseStateFile`, 11 new tests, + the two with side effects); Limpar now goes through `ConfirmReview` with copy that states the real scope (server-sync, sessions only) instead of the old `window.confirm`'s wrong-on-both-counts claim.

🔴 **Two defects found live in the gallery review, neither anticipated by the plan:**
- **The gallery's `FixedFrame`/`ScrollFrame` containment trick contains `AppChrome`'s `position:fixed` sidebar but reserves no lane for it** — in the real app that's `index.css`'s `#root{margin-left:220px}`, which the gallery has nothing equivalent to, so at 768px+ the sidebar rendered ON TOP of the bar's left edge, hiding Início. **User-caught, not self-caught** — reported as "left menu bar is on top of the content bar" after reviewing the first pass of screenshots. Fixed with a `.chromeGutter` class (`Gallery.module.css`, `padding-left:220px` at 768px+) applied only to `AppChrome`'s own cases via two local wrappers (`ChromeFixed`/`ChromeScroll` in `spa.jsx`), mirroring `#root`'s rule without touching the primitive other `.frame` consumers (LogPane, DemoPanel, CheckinSheet) share.
- **The two `ui/Button` icon controls (Sincronizar · Sair) stretched to ~47px against Início's fixed 40px** — `.bar{align-items:stretch}` was applied deliberately (so `.navBtn`'s active underline lands on the bar's own bottom edge) but `align-self:center` was only ever written for `.home`, never passed to the two `Button` instances, so they filled the row's full cross-axis height instead of hugging their `min-height:40px` content. **Also user-caught** ("all three buttons should have the size of the current Início button"). Fixed with a shared `.iconCtrl` class; all three now measure identically 40×40 at both widths, verified via `getBoundingClientRect()`.

**Verified live via Playwright against the local stack** (not by inspection): both defects confirmed present before the fix and absent after (home/sidebar no-overlap proven algebraically — `homeLeft 492 > sidebarRight 480` — after scrolling within the sticky-scroll gallery case, not just at rest); all 7 gallery cases × all 4 themes × both widths reviewed and screenshotted; Criador's `scrollToEditor` still lands correctly; Config → Dados' save/load/clear round-trip exercised end to end including the `ConfirmReview` cancel path. 759 tests (748 baseline + 11 new) / lint 0 at `--max-warnings 0` / format clean / `build` clean — entry chunk **434.9 → 432.3 kB**, the ~16 storage imports `stateBackup.js` now owns having moved into Config's lazy chunk. `design:cards` regenerated (all 9 component cards + the shared CSS bundle they inline); the palette card is untouched (no token changed).

**Open, deliberately left for a future pass:** `.spa-sb-brand{min-height:52px}` vs the bar's `48px` — the sidebar header and the bar no longer share a top edge on desktop, a cosmetic 4px step the plan flagged and left for the approval gate rather than deciding unilaterally.

**2026-08-05 — #150 Close the anon read surface on four tables no public page reads** · migration `0009_lock_unread_anon_tables.sql`. Tier 4's second row, shipped the day it was filed. Same finding shape as #81/`0006`, which closed `coach_profile` + `locations` and left these behind: **`events`** (the coach's agenda — `label`/`notes`/`athleteIds`/`local`, reached only by the authenticated SPA client), **`templates`**, **`results`** (the legacy v1 blob), **`lb_colors`** (zero references anywhere in `src/` since plans/48 — this finishes #60's server leg).

🔑 **The row's insistence on dumping prod's REAL policies before writing the migration is what made it worth doing, and it found two things a migration written from `0001` would have missed:**
- **A name divergence:** prod's read policy on `templates` is **`"public read templates"`**, not `0001`'s reconstructed `"public read"`. A `DROP POLICY "public read" ON templates` would have succeeded silently and left the table wide open. Both spellings are dropped.
- 🔴 **A write hole nobody had filed:** legacy `results` also carries a permissive **`"public result insert"`** *and* `GRANT ALL … TO anon`, so **the public anon key could still INSERT rows into it**. #7/`0003` closed precisely this on `results_v2` and never touched the v1 table — it had been open the whole time, unnoticed because the table is dead. Closed the same way `0003` did: drop the policy **and** revoke the grant. (The other three need no revoke — `GRANT ALL` is inert without a permissive policy; only `results` had both.)

**Verified live on the local stack, both directions.** Anon: all four now return **0 rows while the service role sees the real data** (so it is a lock, not an empty table), every table a public page actually reads still works (`sessions`/`athletes`/`settings`/`results_v2`/`class_executions`/`goals_data`/`exercise_registry`/`tv_state`), and `INSERT` into legacy `results` is now `permission denied for table results`. Coach: signed in **through the real OTP flow** (Mailpit) as `ze_do_arthur@hotmail.com` and confirmed all six locked tables — the four new ones plus `0006`'s two — still **read**, and `events` still **writes**. That last check is the one that matters: the coach's whole Agenda tab depends on the `is_allowed_user()` policy being `FOR ALL` (no `FOR` clause) and therefore covering SELECT, which is the same mechanism `0006` relies on.

⚠️ **Local-only so far — needs the standalone SQL applied to prod + `migration repair --status applied 0009`**, same workflow as `0006`/`0007`/`0008` (a `db push` would drag `0005`). ⚠️ This does **not** make `public:false` sessions private — `sessions` is still one anon-readable blob (**#82**), and `?box=` remains a view filter (**#80**).

**2026-08-05 — #147 `schedule.html`'s three sticky bars become one pinned block** · `2b0a387`. **Tier 4's first row, shipped the day it was filed.** The review found the deep link landing at `y=0` under 193px of chrome; the user, looking at the same page, reported a **second** symptom — the divider under the week nav vanishing on scroll — and both turned out to be the same root cause, along with a **third** nobody had noticed.

`.hdr`/`.selBar`/`.weekNav` each pinned themselves with a hand-written `top` (`0` / `88px` / `136px`), so every bar had to know the others' rendered heights. Two of those numbers were already wrong:
- **`136px` vs a real `140px`** (`.hdr` 88 + `.selBar` 52) — so `.weekNav` jumped up 4px on pin and, at `z-index:19` over `.selBar`'s `18`, **painted across `.selBar`'s bottom divider**. That is the line the user saw disappear.
- **`.selBar` renders only when `!lockedId`** — so on an athlete's own `?id=` link `.weekNav` pinned **48px below** the header, and day-card content scrolled through the gap. Reproduced live before the fix (`gapBetweenHdrAndWeekNav: 48`, `elementFromPoint` in the gap → `_dayBody_`). This is the link athletes actually use.

Fix: one `.stickyHead` wrapper around all three, per-bar `position`/`top`/`z-index` deleted — the same pattern `criador.module.css` already uses, and it makes the stacking the browser's problem instead of a comment's. #147's own scroll offset then **measures that wrapper's height** rather than a literal, which is what makes it correct in both the 193px and 145px cases; `Results.jsx` does the equivalent against its single `<header>`. ⚠️ **The bars moved out of `<main>` to sit in the wrapper** — a no-op on desktop (they are `.mobileOnly`) but required, since `position:sticky` is clipped by an ancestor's box.

**Verified live at 390 and 1280, not by inspection:** rects identical at scroll 0 and scroll 700 (no jump), `.selBar`/`.weekNav` adjacent instead of overlapping (140/140), both dividers visible while scrolling, the `?id=` gap at 0, the deep-linked card 8px clear of the chrome with `_sessName_` — "Eagles Monday Test" — as its first painted line instead of the tail of an Aquecimento block, and the desktop three-pane layout unchanged. 748 tests / lint 0.

🔑 **The user's question at the same moment — "schedule and results use different components to show the same information?" — is the real lesson here, and the answer is yes, three of them:** `Schedule.jsx` has its own `.dayCard` and imports **zero** `shared/` components; `Results.jsx` has a *separate* `.dayCard` in its own module plus `SessionCard`→`AccordionCard`; `index/rail.jsx` has a third via `WeekGrid`/`DaySessionCard`. #51 collapsed the read-only pieces and **#17** deliberately left Schedule's interactive markup alone — which is exactly why a fix aimed at this flow landed on Results and not Schedule. Recorded against **#17** and **#144**.

**2026-08-05 — #142 + #143 Per-box theme + a public Tema page** · [plans/67](./plans/67-per-box-theme.md) · `7f09fcc`.

**#142 was found while researching #143 and turned out to be live data loss.** `saveSettings` (`storage.js:174-177`) is `cacheLS` + `dbSaveSettings` — **a blind overwrite, no merge** — and every other caller compensates by read-merging at the call site (`Config.jsx:36`, `useBoxWarnings.js:18`, whose comment even says *"mirrors Config.jsx so theme/gymName survive"*, `BlockEditor.jsx`, `App.jsx`, `LeaderboardView.jsx`). **`Publicador.jsx:138-188` did not**, and it is seeded from `loadSettings()` so it **fired on mount**: merely opening the tab rewrote the blob from its own 45 export-styling keys. 🔑 **Reproduced live before fixing rather than argued from the code** — with the pre-fix file restored and the tab opened once, the local blob went **54 keys → 48**, losing `theme`, `boxThemes`, `gymSub`, `logo`, `customBenchmarks` and **`boxWarnings`** (#53, a shipped feature the coach uses). ⚠️ **It also blanked `gymName` and `label` to `''`, a facet the row never named:** Publicador doesn't only drop keys it doesn't know about, it overwrites ones it *does* with its own possibly-empty local state. Fixed with the `...loadSettings()` spread the other five callers use **plus** a `useRef` mount guard — both halves are needed, since the spread alone still re-upserts the whole blob and stamps a fresh `updated_at` on every tab open (#109/#111's read-path-never-writes class). ⚠️ **Deliberately NOT fixed by making `saveSettings` merge** — that changes semantics for every caller and makes deleting a key impossible.

**#143** replaced a per-device-only theme (a bare `cone_theme` string, one switcher in the SPA) with a three-source resolution. **The per-box theme lives in `settings.value.boxThemes`, NOT on the location row** — `0006_lock_business_reads.sql` dropped anon read on `locations`, so a public page cannot read a location object at all; same reasoning `useBoxWarnings.js:6-7` already records for #53. The gym-wide default goes in **`settings.value.theme`, which finally has a writer** — `Index.jsx:51-61` had been *reading* that key since forever while nothing in the repo ever wrote it. 🔑 **The design turns on `cone_theme_user` being a SEPARATE key from `cone_theme`:** `cone_theme` keeps its exact prior meaning ("currently applied"), so **the inline boot script duplicated in all 11 HTML entries is untouched**, and the new key is what lets a box default reach a first-time visitor without ever overriding someone who has chosen. New `public/lib/theme.js` (pure, client-free, 21 tests) owns `THEMES` — **canonical now; it had been duplicated three ways and already drifted** ("Spirit Blossom" vs "Spirit Blossom Dark" in the gallery copy) — plus `applyTheme`, `resolveTheme` and `syncTheme`. Precedence is visitor pick → box default → gym default → `totk-dark`, and **an unknown id falls THROUGH at every level** rather than being applied, so a retired theme or a hand-edited blob can't put a `theme-<garbage>` class on `<html>` and drop the page onto the `:root` fallback with nothing explaining why. `syncTheme()` replaced `Index.jsx`'s inline regex rewrite of `documentElement.className` and went into all 6 public pages; **`tv.html` is deliberately out of scope** (no box scope, driven by TvController, and its colours are #97). New `tema.html` + a **third Nav-sheet tile** beside Timer and Coach, routed through `hrefFor` so `?box=`/`?id=` propagate (#124/plans/59's contract), plus the desktop `.sideExtra` link — without it desktop has no route to the page, since the sheet is `display:none` at ≥768px. Registered in `vite.public.config.js` **and** `sw.js` with `CACHE_VERSION` → `cone-v8`; ⚠️ `cache.addAll` **rejects atomically on a 404** so those must ship together, and a comment now says so at the `sw.js` call site.

**Two things changed during execution.** (1) `ThemeCards.jsx` was **split out of `Tema.jsx`** so the gallery can render it — `Tema.jsx` imports `supabaseClient` and the gallery has no backend; same split `rail.jsx` makes for index.html. (2) 🔴 **The plan assumed each preview could carry its own `theme-*` class. It can't** — themes.css scopes every palette to **`html.theme-*`**, so a preview nested in the page cannot inherit the theme it is previewing, and all four must render at once on a page that is only one of them. Resolved with fixed-hex preview classes mirroring themes.css, which is the **same exemption `Config.module.css`'s `.swatchTotkDark` set already carries** (identity colors that must look the same whatever theme is active) — recorded in both files and in a dedicated gallery case, so it doesn't read as drift later.

**Live-verified** end to end against the local stack (service worker + `cone-v*` cache cleared first) at **390 and 1280**: the coach set Eagles → Spirit Blossom Dark in Config → Salvar → `me.html?box=<eagles>` rendered in it, with `boxWarnings` and all 53 other keys intact through the save; two other boxes resolved to their own themes; a visitor pick beat both and survived navigation across pages; "Usar o tema do box" cleared it and returned to the box default, with the hint naming that box's actual theme; the mobile sheet showed **three** tiles all carrying `?box=`. `public-dist/tema.html` confirmed present after `build:all` — **the failure mode there is silent** (a missing build-input entry produces no error, just a 404 in prod). **725 tests (+21) / lint 0 / `build:all` clean / `design:cards` regenerated** (9 component cards + the new `tema.html`; 2 of the 3 changed lines outside the intended Nav diff were #114 date drift — `index.html`'s "today" highlight and `me.html`'s `4 Ago` → `5 Ago` from `BodySheet.jsx:42` — and were reverted, same precedent as plans/65/#24/plans/60).

⚠️ **Observed, not filed:** `Me.jsx` `replaceState`s to `me.html?id=<athlete>` on load, dropping `?box=` from the URL. Benign — `getBoxScope()` has already persisted the scope by then, so both the theme and the session filter survive, including for a first-time visitor following a shared link.

**2026-08-04 — #134 + #135 + #136 ScoreFields polish, round two** · [plans/66](./plans/66-scorefields-polish-2.md) · `e740066`. Three small rows from the 2026-08-03 targeted review, batched because they touch one component and one stylesheet. **#134, first half (the `--border`/`--divider` token gap):** already closed by #137/plans/65 — `.notesToggle` stays on `--divider`, the score `<input>`s are on `--border`, now genuinely 1.50:1 apart per theme. **#134, second half (the wall of always-visible toggles):** a 9-exercise block used to render 9 full-width toggle rows the instant the scale wasn't RX, whether or not the athlete had anything to note — the same "N always-open boxes" problem #116's own comment argued against, one level up. The per-exercise list (`ExerciseNotesRows`, `ScoreFields.jsx`) is now gated behind one outer **"O que foi adaptado?"** toggle, same `.checkpointToggle` visual language as CheckpointFields' "Não terminei"/"Onde parou" — collapsed by default, and *derived* open (`showAll || rows.length > 0`, re-evaluated every render, not a one-time initial state) whenever a real note already exists, so a mounted form reused for a different athlete/block can never hide a real note behind a closed toggle. Closing the outer toggle only hides the list — it never calls `commit`, so notes typed earlier survive being hidden and reappear on reopen; only the per-row toggle keeps the existing "close discards" contract. Each per-exercise row also gained a trailing chevron (`IconChevronDown`/`IconChevronUp` from `@tabler/icons-react`, flipped by `aria-pressed` — the same disclosure convention `AccordionCard` already established, not a new one), addressing the plan's "a toggle should not read as a field even when the border is right": a chevron is an affordance no `<input>` has, independent of border colour. **#135:** re-grepped the `.sm` census fresh rather than trusting the plan's 2026-08-04 numbers — unchanged, confirmed **only `.sm .scaleBtn`** has a production consumer (`ClassPanel.jsx`'s two `<ScaleRow size="sm">` roster rows). Deleted the other 10 dead rules and the gallery's `ScoreInputsSmDemo` case (plus its now-unused `ScaleRow`/`ScoreInputs` named imports in `gallery/groups/shared.jsx` — the default `ScoreFields` import stays, used by every other case). Recorded in the CSS that `ClassPanel`'s compact time input was never affected either way: `TimeField` takes no `size` prop at all, so that row was always styled directly by `tvController.module.css`'s `.editTimeInput` — a stated choice now, not an accident. **#136:** (a) the success modal's DNF label now reuses the confirm modal's exact expression (`isTimeBlock(btype) ? (hasPerfTime ? 'Tempo' : 'Resultado') : 'Resultado'`) via a new `hasPerfTime` field threaded onto `successData`, rather than writing a third copy of the guard — a capped For Time athlete now reads "Resultado", not "Tempo" over a row with no time in it. **2026-08-04 — #137 `--border` ≢ `--divider`, and three failing contrast cells** · [plans/65](./plans/65-border-divider-tokens.md) · `5527586`. Decision taken at planning: **differentiate**, not drop the claim. `--border` is now derived **per palette** (two of the four are light, where "stronger" means *darker*) as the point on that palette's own `--div → --muted` ramp measuring **1.50:1 against `--divider`** in every theme — totk-dark `#4a3e30` · totk-light `#b4a490` · spirit-blossom `#4a3060` · sb-light `#c4a8d8`, landing at **1.80–1.98:1 against `--bg`** where `--divider` sits at 1.18–1.33. ⚠️ **`--divider` did not move**: it is ~330 of the ~360 call sites and moving it would restyle the whole app. **Token count verified programmatically still 29 per theme, identical key sets across all four** — both tokens already existed, nothing added or removed (#43 is where that count becomes load-bearing). **(b) `--red`/`--err`** (always the same value, and used as text/border only — never as a fill, grepped) measured **3.67:1 (totk-dark) / 3.69:1 (spirit-blossom)** on `--bg` and, on the surface `SessionTextPane`'s `.warnRow` actually renders on, **3.23 / 3.35** — the *least* legible text in a pane whose textarea is 14.8:1, and the only element telling the user something needs attention. Raised on the **two dark themes only** (the lights already passed) by an **exact per-channel scalar** — `#c83030` ×1.25 → `#fa3c3c`, `#c82850` ×1.20 → `#f03060` — so the hue is mathematically unchanged; now 4.58–5.40 on every surface. **(c) The one cell plans/57 deliberately deferred here** — `.rpeBtn.on`'s `--accent-text`-on-`--teal` at **3.80:1** in spirit-blossom-light, a pairing `Button`'s primary variant shares — is fixed **in `--teal` itself** (`#1490a0` → `#0f727e`, carried to its equal `--accent`/`--cyan`), which took that cell to **5.63** *and* cleared the same token's separate `--teal`-as-body-text failure (3.34 → **4.95**, one of #14's cells) from one edit. That is **not automatically a bug** — "containers and inputs strong, inner chips and KPIs hairline" reads coherently, and `AccordionCard`'s `.card`(divider)/`.expanded`(border) pair got *better* — so only the **two shared primitives whose mismatch is visible side by side** were reclassified: `ui/Input`'s `.control` and `shared/MaskedTimeInput`'s `.control`, both form controls, both → `--border`. **2026-08-04 — #132 + #133 The logging chain's review step (a composed-chain bug + three forked confirm frames)** · [plans/64](./plans/64-logging-confirm-chain.md) · `e47dec8`. **#133 absorbed #132** — same three files (`Results.jsx` · `LogPane.jsx` · `DeskRegPane.jsx`), same markup, so the note rows landed while the frame was being replaced rather than in a second pass. All three now render the shared `ConfirmReview` (#54/C0, built specifically to collapse these three forks and never adopted until now) with the canonical **"Revisar registro" / Editar / Confirmar** — `Results.jsx` keeps its real "Confirmar alteração" distinction on an edit via a `title` override, so that wasn't lost to the default. **#132:** the #116 per-exercise adaptation note — the field that records *what* the athlete changed — was invisible in every confirm and success step on all three surfaces, even though it read back correctly everywhere else (`LoggedResult`, a re-log prefill). A pure composed-chain bug: no single plan's isolated verification (plans/52–56 each verified their own step; #116 verified only the input) could have caught a gap in how the pieces compose. Fixed by rendering `exerciseRows` through `ReadBox`/`ReadRow` in every confirm and success body — **`LogPane`'s success step had no read-back at all before this** (just an icon/title/link), so it gained the same score+note detail the other two surfaces show, which is what "every success step" required. **Dead CSS deleted** after a zero-consumer grep: `deskConfirmBox`/`deskConfirmRow`/`deskCancelBtn`/`deskConfirmBtn` (shared by `LogPane` and `DeskRegPane`, so it only went once both converted) and `Results.module.css`'s `confirm*` family. 🔴 **Two things found at execution, neither in the plan:** (1) **`.logPane` carries a CSS `transform` even at rest** (`translateX(100%)`/`(0)` — never `none`), which makes it a containing block for `position:fixed` descendants; a nested `<ConfirmReview>` would silently position itself relative to the pane instead of the viewport, so both `LogPane` and `DeskRegPane` render it as a **sibling outside** the pane element. (2) **Keeping the pane "behind" the dialog required restructuring, not just a markup swap** — the confirm step used to *replace* the form's DOM via a ternary, which would have discarded scroll position and field state the instant the dialog opened, the same class of bug one level up from what the plan was fixing. Both files now render the form unconditionally (gated only on `success`, never on the confirm step) and layer `ConfirmReview` as an always-present sibling controlled by `open` — verified live via an unchanged `scrollTop` (263) across open→Escape. **Two scope calls recorded rather than made silently:** `Results.jsx`'s confirm-modal scale/opacity transition was deliberately **not** ported into the shared component (would have changed `ConfirmReview`'s mount lifecycle for every future consumer to save one surface's animation — a comment at the CSS site says so; the success modal keeps its own transition, untouched); and `ConfirmReview.module.css`'s `.body` became a flex column with `gap` (was `margin-bottom` only) so multiple `ReadBox` pairs — score + notes, or several blocks in `LogPane`'s multi-block confirm — space evenly without a per-consumer wrapper. 704 tests / lint 0 (`--max-warnings 0`) / `build:all` clean / `format` clean. ✅ **Live-verified against the local stack with real prod-shaped data** (Playwright, 1280 and 390): edited Arthur's real SC-scale "Eagles"-week For Time result (carrying a genuine note, "Fiz com banda auxiliar" on Toes to Bar) through all three surfaces — the note rendered in confirm **and** success on `DeskRegPane` (desktop sidebar), `LogPane` (mobile drawer, including its 2-block For Time+EMOM confirm with independent `ReadBox`/note pairs), and `Results.jsx` (which also correctly showed "Confirmar alteração" on this edit path); Escape on both `LogPane` and `DeskRegPane` returned to the still-scrolled, still-filled form rather than closing the pane; zero console errors on any surface throughout. Gallery (`ConfirmReview`, `ScoreFields`, `LogPane`, `DeskRegPane` cases, including their pre-built "erro no envio" states) rendered clean — no `design:cards` run, since no gallery-rendered component's markup shape changed.

**2026-08-04 — #139 + #140 Two Criador inputs that rewrote what the coach typed, per keystroke** · [plans/63](./plans/63-criador-keystroke-writes.md). One root cause, two symptoms: normalization work done on every keystroke that belongs at commit time. **#139's "two candidate fixes, pick deliberately" resolved to BOTH** — they are complements, not alternatives, and each alone leaves a live hole: `saveS` cannot cover the Texto pane (it serializes **unsaved** blocks) and `serializeGoal` cannot cover `toSecs`/`goalStr`/`goalOutcome` (they never go through the serializer). Shipped: new pure `normalizeGoal`/`normalizeBlockGoals` (`blockModel.js`) running `expandMMSS` over a `kind:'time'` goal, applied **last** in `saveS`'s existing `materializeBlocks(normalizeLegacyCardio(…))` pipeline; and `serializeGoal` completing `min`/`max` before its own `short()`, so an unblurred `14` emits `Meta: 14'` instead of a bare `Meta: 14` that `parseGoal` hands back as `{kind:'text'}` — the shape that silently kills #117's badge. **#140** (user-reported same day): `BlockEditor.jsx:401` ran `.trim()` on **every keystroke**, so the trailing space of `Aquecimento ` was stripped before the next character arrived — the only trim-on-change input in `src/`. Fixed with a local draft committing raw on change and normalizing on blur, which also closes the second defect in that line (`customName` derives to `''` when `label === type`, blanking the field mid-word). 🔴 **The draft had to sync against what the field LAST SENT UP, not against the derived prop** — the parent transforms what this field writes, so a plain `prev !== prop` sync resyncs to `''` on that very keystroke and defeats the draft in exactly the case it exists for; caught by reasoning through "WOD" typed into a WOD block, not by a test. ⚠️ **`expandMMSS` zero-pads and `parseTimeToken` strips the zero again**, so a single-digit goal makes one padding shift (`'9'` → `"sub 09'"` → `'9:00'`) and is stable after — pinned as the "stabilizes, not byte-identical" standard `audit-text-roundtrip.mjs` already uses, not "fixed". **Live-verified** at 1280 against the local stack (no service worker present, checked first): `Aquecimento geral` typed left to right in one pass; clearing falls back to the type; `For Time` typed into a For Time block survives; `14` + flip to `¶ Texto` **without blurring** → `Meta: 14'`; the same unblurred `14` saved as `{kind:'time', min:'14:00'}`; week-grid Texto renders `Meta: 14'`. ✅ **Prod repair applied by the user 2026-08-04 and re-verified: `goal-kind` 1 → 0**, clean round-trip blocks 206 → **207/361**. (Between the code landing and the SQL running the counter read 1 while reporting `"14" → "14:00"` — the fix working, not the bug.) ⚠️ **The re-run surfaced one unrelated thing this plan did not fix** — the audit's separate *"goals com `min` numérico em storage"* counter reads 1: `2026-07-29 #2 AMRAP` holds `{"min": 4, kind:"rounds"}`, a JS **number**. Filed as **#141**. 704 tests (+6) / lint 0 / `build:all` clean.
