**2026-07-03 — Quick-wins batch (#33 · #9 · #13 · #11 · #12)** · `plans/07-quick-wins-batch.md` — five independent S items in one session. **#33** `defaultTurmaLabel()` in `useClassTracking.js` rounds the current time down to the half-hour (`:00`/`:30`, hour never bumped) for the default turma name; `startClass()` also re-primes it after each class so a controller left open across multiple classes back-to-back doesn't offer a stale time (caught by review, not in the original plan). **#9** `.ringInner` in `Timer.module.css` gained `user-select:none`/`-webkit-tap-highlight-color:transparent` to stop the blue text-selection flash when tapping LAP near the round counter. **#13** new `isNavHidden()` export in `Nav.jsx` checks `?from=tv` and early-returns `null`; TV's two QR-code URL builders (`QrFooter`, `QrSlide`) now append `&from=tv`; `Schedule.jsx` (the only page currently reachable via a TV QR code — confirmed by grep) zeroes its `.pageRoot` bottom padding when nav is hidden so no empty gap is left. **#11** in Criador's `ExerciseRow`, the Sets/Reps qty block now renders before the exercise-name input in the desktop inline row (mobile is unaffected — qty already lives only in the bottom sheet there); confirmed no CSS/JS depends on the prior DOM order. **#12** (design-gated) — TV timer slide shows a pulsing countdown badge (10→1) in the final 10 seconds of For Time/Benchmark/Estações blocks (not AMRAP/EMOM); mockup card `design/mockups/07-tv-countdown-pill.html` synced to the Cone Design System project first. An `xhigh`-equivalent `/code-review medium` (8 parallel finder angles + verification) caught a real violation before push: the badge's `border-radius:42px` broke CLAUDE.md's "no border-radius on public pages" rule (TV.jsx is under `src/public/`) — fixed to square corners with a cream border instead, in both the shipped CSS and the design card (re-synced). Added `useClassTracking.test.js` (7 cases) for the half-hour rounding boundary. `npm test` green (79, up from 72) · both builds (`npm run build:all`) succeed · no `/security-review` needed (no RLS/auth/user-input surface). **Manual QA surfaced two pre-existing local-dev-environment gaps**, unrelated to this batch's own changes but blocking testing #33/#13: (1) local OTP login emails only rendered GoTrue's default magic-link template (a dead `{{ .ConfirmationURL }}` link to the wrong port) with no visible code — `LoginScreen.jsx` never uses the link, only `verifyOtp()` on a typed code — fixed with a custom `supabase/templates/magic_link.html` + `config.toml`'s `[auth.email.template.magic_link]`, verified via a live OTP request landing in Mailpit with the code visible. (2) the local `supabase_realtime` publication had **zero tables** in it (`select * from pg_publication_tables` returned 0 rows), so every `.on('postgres_changes', ...)` subscription in the app (TV.jsx's `tv_state`/`results_v2`/`class_executions`, `useClassTracking.js`'s `class_executions`) silently never fired — symptom: a newly created class didn't appear until switching sessions forced a manual `loadClasses()`, and tv.html needed a manual reload to pick up a pushed slide/QR. Fixed via `supabase/migrations/0005_enable_realtime.sql` (idempotent per-table guard, since plain `ALTER PUBLICATION ... ADD TABLE` errors on an existing member and has no native `IF NOT EXISTS`); confirmed prod is unaffected — user confirmed the TV screen already updates live at the gym today, so prod's publication was presumably configured via the dashboard and just never captured into a migration (same class of drift as #34's `class_executions` policies). Verified via `supabase db reset` (0001→0005 replays clean) + reseed + `npm test` (79 green).

**2026-07-03 — #34 class_executions authenticated INSERT/DELETE hardening** · `supabase/migrations/0004_class_exec_auth_hardening.sql` — a live `supabase db dump --linked` revealed prod's real `class_executions` policies were exactly `ce_select_anon` (SELECT), `ce_insert_auth`/`ce_delete_auth` (INSERT/DELETE scoped to `auth.role()='authenticated'`) and — after #7's `0003` dropped `ce_update_anon` — **no UPDATE policy and no `is_allowed_user()` policy at all** (the diff engine's earlier "no schema changes" was untrustworthy for RLS policies; the pg_dump was authoritative). Two problems closed at once: (1) **#34** — open OTP signup meant any self-served `authenticated` (non-coach) session could forge new class rows or delete real ones via the two role-only policies; (2) an **active regression `0003` introduced on prod only** — coach end-class/live-registration/group-rotation are all UPDATEs, left with no permissive policy since `bc2e98a` (local never surfaced it because 0001's reconstruction carries `"auth write"`). Fix: `CREATE POLICY "auth write" FOR ALL USING(is_allowed_user())` (restores coach INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE) + `DROP ce_insert_auth`/`ce_delete_auth`; idempotent across both histories via DROP-IF-EXISTS-then-CREATE for `"auth write"` (prod lacked it, local has it) and IF EXISTS on the two role-only drops (prod had them, local never did). Verified on the local stack by reproducing prod's exact policy set and minting authenticated JWTs against PostgREST — **11/11 checks**: pre-fix an `attacker@example.com` session INSERTed a forged row + DELETEd a real one and the coach UPDATE was blocked (regression reproduced); post-fix attacker INSERT→403 and DELETE→row-survives, coach INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE all work, anon `class_checkin` RPC still appends to the roster, anon direct INSERT/UPDATE→401. `supabase db reset` confirmed `0004` applies cleanly in the 0001→0004 chain; `npm test` green (72). **Shipped to prod** (`supabase db push` — only `0004` applied; `0001`/`0002` repair-marked, `0003` already applied): re-dumped prod post-push → `class_executions` now `auth write` (is_allowed_user, ALL) + `ce_select_anon` (SELECT), `ce_*_auth` gone; migration history 0001–0004 all present. Prod behavioral coach-write check left to a manual app smoke (create + end a class) — no coach session drivable from here and no test writes to prod — but the fix *restores* a path that was broken, so any failure is immediately visible.

**2026-07-03 — #7 Anon write hardening (results_v2 + class_executions)** · `supabase/migrations/0003_anon_write_rpcs.sql` — two SECURITY DEFINER RPCs (`class_checkin` append-only roster join, `log_result` upsert on `UNIQUE(athlete_id, session_id)` that never touches `coach_note`/`flag_for_review`) replace the four anon `.update()`/`.upsert()` call sites in `Schedule.jsx` (check-in, self-log, desktop-reg) and `Results.jsx` (self-log) · `REVOKE INSERT, UPDATE ... FROM anon` on both tables · a `/security-review` pass before pushing caught that this alone was bypassable: the pre-existing `"public result insert"/"public result update"/"public update"` policies (`using(true) with check(true)`) had no `TO` clause, so they applied to *every* role, not just `anon` — and this app's OTP signup is open to any email (`is_allowed_user()` is enforced by policy, not by signup), so an attacker could self-serve an `authenticated` session and PATCH any row directly, fully bypassing the new RPCs. Confirmed live against the local stack (signed up `attacker@example.com`, got a valid `authenticated` JWT, successfully overwrote `coach_note` pre-fix) before dropping the three stale policies — SECURITY DEFINER functions bypass RLS regardless (owner has BYPASSRLS), so dropping them doesn't affect the RPCs, and `"auth write"` (`is_allowed_user()`) remains the only direct-table write path. Re-verified post-fix: anon PATCH/INSERT rejected on both tables, the same attacker JWT now blocked, allowlisted-coach direct writes unaffected, both RPCs round-trip correctly (including upsert keeping the original row id and not letting one athlete's log touch another's row) · `npm test` green (72 tests) · **shipped to prod the same session** (client code depends on the RPCs existing — a static-only GH Pages deploy with no matching DB migration would've broken live self-log/check-in): `supabase link`, then `supabase db diff --linked` surfaced that prod's real schema (built via dashboard, never through the CLI) uses different policy names than `0001_init.sql`'s hand-reconstruction assumed — e.g. `class_executions`'s permissive-update policy is really `ce_update_anon`, not `"public update"` — so `0003`'s `DROP POLICY` statements were rewritten with `IF EXISTS` covering both names before pushing. Prod's CLI migration history was empty (schema predates the migration workflow); `0001`/`0002` marked applied via `supabase migration repair` (metadata-only, not re-executed — replaying them risked `CREATE POLICY` collisions with prod's existing same-purpose objects) so `db push` applied only `0003`. Verified against prod itself post-push (read-only/no-op checks only — no prod service-role key available to write-then-clean-up test rows): anon PATCH/INSERT denied on both tables, both RPCs resolve and execute. Surfaced a related but out-of-scope gap, filed as **#34**: prod's real `class_executions` policies also include `ce_insert_auth`/`ce_delete_auth` scoped to `auth.role() = 'authenticated'` rather than `is_allowed_user()` — since signup is open, a non-coach authenticated session can still forge/delete class rows (not an UPDATE-tampering vector, so out of `#7`'s scope, but a real gap).

**2026-07-03 — #4 Dev environment — local Supabase (Docker)** · `supabase init`'d in `cone/` on ports shifted +10 (54331/54332/54333/54334) since this host already runs another local Supabase stack on the CLI defaults · `supabase/migrations/0001_init.sql` is the new schema source of truth: 11 JSONB blob tables + `results_v2` from the existing root SQL files, plus `tv_state` and `class_executions` hand-reconstructed from CLAUDE.md's documented columns and every call site in `src/` (neither ever had committed SQL before) · discovered and fixed a real gap along the way: the local CLI no longer auto-grants Data API roles (anon/authenticated/service_role) table privileges, so the migration adds explicit `GRANT`s — RLS is unaffected, this is just the coarse layer Postgres needs before RLS is even evaluated · both Supabase clients now read `VITE_SUPABASE_URL`/`VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` from env (`src/public/supabaseClient.js` was the last hardcoded one); `vite.public.config.js` needed an explicit `envDir` since its `root` is the repo root but the env files live in `cone/` · `.env.development` (new, committed — local stack has no real secret boundary) vs `.env.production` (unchanged); `.env.local` deleted (redundant prod-value copy, nothing reads it anymore) · `scripts/seed-dev.mjs` snapshots prod's blob tables + `results_v2` into local via the local service-role key · verified: `npm run dev`/`dev:public` resolve to `127.0.0.1`, `npm run build`/`build:public` still bake in prod, `supabase db reset` + reseed round-trips cleanly, full test suite (72 tests) green · ran an `xhigh` `/code-review` before pushing (per WORKFLOW.md's ritual for L items), which caught real gaps in the hand-reconstruction the plan had already flagged as a residual risk: `allowed_emails` was missing `enable row level security` (the new blanket GRANT would've given anon full CRUD on the table `is_allowed_user()` trusts — closed, verified anon SELECT/INSERT both now blocked against the local stack), `submit_pr`/`clear_pr` RPCs (`supabase-rpcs.sql`, one level above `cone/` — missed because it wasn't inside `cone/`) were absent from the migration (added as `0002_rpcs.sql`, both RPCs smoke-tested end to end via REST), local GoTrue defaulted to 6-digit OTP while LoginScreen.jsx expects 8 (`otp_length = 8` now set and confirmed live), plus `is_allowed_user()`/the new RPCs got `search_path` pinned and the GRANT got an `alter default privileges` companion so future migrations don't reintroduce the same gap. Unblocks #7's RLS probe (deferred to a future session — this migration reproduces the `results_v2`/`class_executions` anon-write gap faithfully, doesn't fix it).

**2026-07-03 — #3 timer.html — desktop running layout** · Sidebar offset, `vh`-scaled ring, and side-by-side exercise list on the running/paused screens were already built (commit f645054) — the actual gap was `.wrap` never getting a base `display:flex;flex-direction:column;height:100%` rule, so it never stretched to fill `#root`'s `100dvh` flex column. Every screen (cfg/ready/running/paused/finished) fell back to content-height, leaving a large blank void below the content on tall/desktop viewports (visible on mobile too, just less obviously). One-line fix in `Timer.module.css`; no mockup needed since the already-approved desktop design just wasn't rendering — confirmed via Playwright screenshots at 1280×800 and 390×844 across all screens.

### Decisions recorded
- **2026-07-21 — Criador jumps the C-queue, and takes text entry with it.** Coach feedback: he writes the whole week as free text in a phone notepad and then re-types it into the app, and he can't compare days without a second window. **(a) Queue order.** The design program's C1→C5 sequence is *convenience, not dependency* — only C0 gates C1–C5, and it shipped. So **C4 (#58, Criador) runs before C1–C3 (#55/#56/#57)**, which stay queued behind it unchanged. **(b) Three sessions, not one** ([plans/35](./plans/35-criador-decomposition.md) → [36](./plans/36-criador-text-mode.md) → [37](./plans/37-design-c4-criador.md)): #26's decomposition first (2063 lines, zero tests, and both later sessions rewrite large parts of it), then the text feature, then the layout/design pass. **(c) Text is a projection, not storage** — the parser produces the same block objects the app already stores, so TV / schedule / results / Publicador are untouched; the toggle is editor UI state and is never persisted. **(d) The parser never drops a line** — unclassified lines are preserved verbatim in `block.notes` and surfaced in the preview. This is the property that makes it safe to hand a coach's week to a regex. **(e) No LLM** — deterministic grammar only, so it is testable and offline. **(f) The block type picker is not redesigned** — the coach calls it spot on; #58 gives it token/a11y touch-ups only. **(g) `block.duration` is not converted to mm:ss with the rest of #35** — it is stored as bare minutes and `toSecs('14')` reads that as *seconds*, so a partial conversion silently corrupts caps; split out as **#93**.
- **2026-07-19 — #54 C0: the SPA design standard is settled (Phase 1 synced, awaiting approval).** Full spec + zoo census in [plans/33](./plans/33-design-c0-spa-standard.md) "Phase 1 deliverable"; mockup `cone/design/mockups/28-spa-standard-c0.html` (4-theme switcher, verified). Key settled calls C1–C5 must apply: **button hierarchy** = primary(`--accent`) / secondary(outline `--border`) / destructive(`--red`) / ghost(text) × sizes md·40/sm·32/xs·24, hover via `filter:brightness`/`color-mix` (never baked hex), icon-only **requires** an `aria-label` prop. **Destructive = `--red`** — it *exists* in all 4 themes, so no new danger token (the plan's premise was wrong); `--err` stays named for validation. **Input** = real `<label htmlFor>` + one `:focus-visible` ring (`outline:2px --accent`), 16px value; `MaskedTimeInput` (net-new #35, mm:ss auto-colon, `--font-mono`). **Confirm policy** = one `ConfirmReview` `role="dialog"` shell (focus-trap · Escape→form · backdrop `.7`) with canonical labels **"Revisar registro"/"Revisar alteração" · "Editar" · "Confirmar"** (kills the 3 forks: Results "Confirmar registro"/"Confirmar", DeskRegPane "Registrar ✓", LogPane "Confirmar ✓"). **Spacing `--sp-1..5` = 4/8/12/16/24 + `--radius-sm:4`/`--radius-md:6`** go in `themes.css :root` (theme-invariant, so public/shared inherit). **Replace, not wrap** — each page deletes `.b`/`.tb-btn` globals as it adopts. **Locations:** `ConfirmReview`/`MaskedTimeInput` → `src/public/shared/`; `Button`/`Input`/`Card` → new `src/components/ui/` + gallery "SPA / UI" group (must import no Supabase client — no CI gate catches a broken gallery import, so open `gallery.html`).
- **2026-07-18 — #53 Part B: box warnings live in `settings.value.boxWarnings`, NOT on `locations`.** `settings` is anon-readable and the index already fetches it; **`locations` can't be used — #81 locked it to `is_allowed_user()` reads and the index is anon.** Write is read-merge-write via `saveSettings` (mirrors `Config.jsx`) so theme/gymName aren't clobbered. **Shape evolved to a dated list** (the redesign, prints 02/03): `[{ id, date, message, box, active }]` where `box` is a `locationId` or `'all'` (gym-wide). The index shows the **3 most recent** active in-scope ones (desktop strip) / 1 (mobile); the message's part before `' — '` is bolded. Criador's input is a small list manager (add / date / message / on-off / delete) scoped to the existing `selBox` selector. (Superseded the initial per-box single `{ [key]: {message, active} }` object; the reader treats a non-array as empty.)
- **2026-07-18 — #53 index architecture: a full-width week grid drives a selected-day panel.** `selectedDate` (default today) is the one piece of state; clicking a `WeekGrid` day swaps the two-column panel below (`DaySessionCard` flex 1.3 + `DayRanking` 340px) and the day-title. The week's results are fetched in **one** `results_v2 .in(session_id, weekIds)` query (feeds both the ranking and the per-session counts) — no per-day round-trip. Blocks are individually expandable (shared `ExerciseList` `size="tiny"`). Replaced the earlier right-rail build (`WeekStrip`/`TodayRanking`/`BoxNotice`) after the user evolved the Design in Claude Design (prints 02/03).
- **2026-07-18 — #53 TV finale (2·A + 4·C, greenlit from mockup 27).** 2·A: `WodSlide` flags ≤2-block sessions and renders them `big` (large `ExerciseList` + badge) with the grid taking full height + centering rows (`.wodBlocksFew`), so a lone WOD fills the wall; the scale-**down** effect stays as a safety cap and ≥3 blocks are unchanged. 4·C: a theme-invariant `--font-mono` token (system mono stack) in `themes.css` replaces `'Courier New'` at the 5 TV sites + the index rail's `.rkPerf`; pairs with #54.
- **2026-07-18 — #53 Part B: Nav icons are `@tabler/icons-react`, never the `ti` webfont.** The webfont isn't loaded on `leaderboard.html` and Nav is shared by every public page; the React components inline SVG with `stroke:currentColor` so they tint with the theme. `.ovIc` needs an explicit `color` (the `<a>.ovTile` sets none, so the SVG would otherwise inherit the UA link blue).
- **2026-07-18 — #53 Part A: Benchmark block-type color = amber (canonical `blkColor`).** The timer `BlockTypePicker` painted the Benchmark *type* card `#d05878` (rose) while `blkColor` (wod.js) classifies Benchmark in the **amber** family (same as For Time / AMRAP / EMOM). Aligned the picker card to `var(--gold)` to match both `blkColor` and its amber-family siblings. The `#d05878` rose survives only as the **Girls** benchmark-*subcategory* accent (a category decoration, not a block-family color).
- **2026-07-18 — #53 Part A: TV countdown-pill red (`#c84038`) stays a hardcoded status/data color — NOT tokenized.** It's the exact value the timer ring uses for its final-seconds danger state (`ringCol`, slides.jsx). Tokenizing the pill to `var(--red)` would drift it from the still-hardcoded ring on light themes. Exempt like the block families / scales — saturated enough to read on every theme's background. (Plan flagged `TV.module.css:127/133/134/135` for the rgb sweep; kept them as-is, tokenized only the gold glow at `:196` → `color-mix(var(--gold))`.)
- **2026-07-18 — #53 Part A: `index.html` `theme-color` meta is theme-aware via a post-stylesheet inline script** reading `--stone` (the sticky header bg), not a hardcoded per-theme map (which would duplicate themes.css). The static `#161210` value is the no-JS fallback.
- **2026-07-09 — #27/#28 restructured into the unified design-pass program.** One analysis pass covered both surfaces (public + SPA); execution is 10 bundled sessions (#49–#59, see [plans/16](./plans/16-design-pass-program.md)) instead of 18 per-page ones, each folding in its own slice of #15 (tokens), #14 (mechanical a11y), and #18 (fixed-height layout — **#18 retired as a standalone row**, it lives entirely inside the B-sessions). #25/#26 execute as the first halves of #59/#58. Program precedes #43 (themes need the token-clean codebase).
- **2026-07-09 — Border-radius rule: Option A (circles exempt).** `border-radius: 50%` (true circles: timer ring, avatar badges, dots — 19 in public, 27 full-src) is a shape primitive and stays; everything else including pills (`999px` ends) is a rounded rect and gets squared on public pages. Recorded in CLAUDE.md design section; per-page enforcement in the B/C sessions.
- **2026-07-09 — Font-weights: load real Cinzel 500/800 (first session that touches them adds `@fontsource/cinzel/{500,800}.css`); Amarante ships only weight 400 upstream, so all Spirit Blossom bolds are browser-synthesized by design — not a bug, don't chase it.**
- **Estações render divergence — INTENTIONAL, not drift (2026-07-05).** TV (`slides.jsx` `BlockCard`) flattens stations into one flat exercise list — a glanceable wall display where station grouping doesn't matter at a glance. `Schedule.jsx` (`BlockDetail`, `:507-517`) keeps full station structure (header + grouped exercises) as the canonical detailed view, since a coach/athlete reading the page benefits from knowing which exercises belong to which station. Comment added at the TV flatten site (`slides.jsx`). No code change needed on the Schedule side.
- **Estações — KEEP.** Reviewed removal blast radius (dedicated render branch, CSS, TV paths, existing saved `type:'Estações'` data); not worth removing.
- **Estações** is woven through Criador, `Schedule.jsx` (`stations` loop, `restBetweenCycles`, `stationsCapMins()`), CSS `.detailStation*`, and likely TV — leave intact.
- **2026-07-02** — review numbering: rows renumbered #5–#31 after inserting #4 (dev env) and merging review findings; old #5 (mobile pass) merged into #27 (UX walk).
